EN| Did you know that our Sun is a very unusual star?
ZH| 你知道吗？我们的太阳是一颗非常不寻常的恒星。

EN| To start with, it’s a yellow dwarf.
ZH| 首先嘛，它是一颗黄矮星。

EN| Those constitute for just 7-10%% of all stars in the universe.
ZH| 而这类恒星在宇宙所有恒星中只占7-10%%。

EN| \nIt's an outlier even among those.
ZH| \n就算在黄矮星里，它也算个异类。

EN| Most yellow dwarves are primarily covered with dark spots.
ZH| 大多数黄矮星的表面都布满黑子。

EN| The Sun isn’t.
ZH| 太阳却不是。

EN| The majority of its surface is bright – in fact, it has three times the number of light spots than it does dark spots.
ZH| 它的表面大部分是明亮的——事实上，它的光斑数量是黑子的三倍。

EN| Sure, its overall brightness index is nothing special.
ZH| 诚然，它的整体亮度指数平平无奇。

EN| You could even call it underwhelming compared to other stars of its type.
ZH| 甚至可以说，和同类恒星比起来，它有点拿不出手。

EN| But even so, it works harder than most to shine as bright as it can.
ZH| 但即便如此，它仍比大多数恒星都更努力地燃烧，只为尽可能闪耀。

EN| And I find that really cute.
ZH| 而我觉得，这特别可爱。

EN| I don’t remember where I read about this.
ZH| 我不记得自己是从哪儿读到的这些了。

EN| Must have been some newspaper from years ago.
ZH| 应该是很多年前某份报纸上的东西吧。

EN| But that small piece of trivia still pops in my mind whenever I look up at the morning sky.
ZH| 可每当抬头望向清晨的天空，那点小小的冷知识总会浮现在我脑海里。

EN| It’s not much of a view, to be honest.
ZH| 说实话，这算不上什么风景。

EN| Not much of a sky, not much of a sun.
ZH| 算不上天空，也算不上太阳。

EN| I guess that’s the kind of region it is.
ZH| 大概，我们这地方就是这样吧。

EN| Some people say the sun is just lazing around in our parts, what with the constant snow.
ZH| 有人说，太阳在我们这一带就是在偷懒，毕竟这里老是下雪。

EN| But people that go around saying that, well...
ZH| 不过，张口闭口说这种话的人啊……

EN| \nThey clearly haven’t read that newspaper.
ZH| \n他们显然没读过那份报纸。

EN| The more I thought about things like that and the more I learned, the more I realised how incredible everything is.
ZH| 这类事情想得越多、学得越多，我就越发觉得，万事万物都不可思议。

EN| I'm not just saying that to mean “amazing”.
ZH| 我这么说，可不只是“好厉害”那种意思。

EN| I mean “improbable”.
ZH| 我说的是“不太可能”。

EN| You can't deny the world is full of great coincidences.
ZH| 不可否认，这个世界充满了绝妙的巧合。

EN| It's to the point where I've begun to ask myself how much of it we can attribute to pure chance.
ZH| 巧合多到我开始自问：这一切里，究竟有多少能归功于纯粹的偶然。

EN| The universe was so kind as to give us both a star and a planet with the perfect conditions for sentient life.
ZH| 宇宙是如此仁慈，既给了我们一颗恒星，又给了我们一颗具备智慧生命完美条件的行星。

EN| Just the slightest fluctuation in the composition of our atmosphere, the size of the globe, or the distance between Earth and the Sun – and none of us, none of the beauty surrounding us, would exist.
ZH| 只要大气成分、地球体积、抑或日地距离有丝毫偏差——我们、连同环绕着我们的这一切美景，都将不复存在。

EN| Some would call it a miracle.
ZH| 有人会称之为奇迹。

EN| And of course, that's a lovely way to put it...
ZH| 当然啦，这说法很动听……

EN| But isn't that giving the world we're living in too little credit?
ZH| 可这是不是太小看我们身处的这个世界了？

EN| Ah, but it’s not that I subscribe to the anthropic principle or anything!
ZH| 啊，不过我可不是在鼓吹什么人择原理！

EN| I'm not so arrogant as to believe the universe exists just for our sake.
ZH| 我还没自大到认为宇宙是为了我们才存在的地步。

EN| Or rather, I'm not so sure it exists solely to be observed.
ZH| 或者说，我也不确定它是否只是为了被观察而存在。

EN| When a baby is born, it's not preoccupied with how it is perceived.
ZH| 婴儿刚出生时，并不会在意别人怎么看自己。

EN| That conscious need emerges later, as the child develops self-awareness.
ZH| 那种有意识的渴望，是随着孩子萌生自我意识才出现的。

EN| Until that point, its curiosity is aimed at its surroundings.
ZH| 在那之前，它的好奇心全都指向周遭的世界。

EN| So if anything, it's the opposite of what the anthropic principle entails.
ZH| 所以说，这恰恰与人择原理的推论相反。

EN| We're social creatures, so the need to be observed makes sense for our survival.
ZH| 我们是社会性生物，“被关注”的需求关乎我们的生存，说得通。

EN| But to say the universe itself shares that priority...
ZH| 但要说宇宙本身也把这种需求当回事……

EN| It just doesn't make sense to me.
ZH| 我怎么都想不通。

EN| \nAlthough, you can also think about it this way.
ZH| \n不过嘛，也可以换个角度想。

EN| A child is curious about the world because it perceives the world as external.
ZH| 孩子对世界好奇，是因为在孩子眼里，世界是身外之物。

EN| But what if the child {i}is{/i} the world?
ZH| 可如果孩子{i}就是{/i}世界本身呢？

EN| What if all that it could ever observe existed within itself?
ZH| 如果它所能观察到的一切，都存在于它自身之内呢？

EN| What can the all-encompassing universe be curious about?
ZH| 无所不包的宇宙，还能对什么感到好奇？

EN| There’s nothing but itself, so nothing but itself!
ZH| 除了它自己什么都没有，所以好奇的也只有它自己！

EN| There’s nothing else to care about in the whole wide world!
ZH| 整个世界里，根本没有别的可操心的事！

EN| It is the world!
ZH| 因为它就是世界啊！

EN| Duh!
ZH| 这不是明摆着嘛！

EN| Ah, wait, now this makes perfect sense.
ZH| 啊，等等，这么说一切都说得通了。

EN| If I put it like that...
ZH| 要这么讲的话……

EN| I guess the universe has no choice but to be self-centered.
ZH| 宇宙大概也别无选择，只能以自我为中心了。

EN| Even if this wasn’t the case when it was born...
ZH| 就算它诞生之初并非如此……

EN| What if the universe developed a desire to be perceived by others as it matured?
ZH| 随着宇宙渐渐“长大”，它会不会也萌生了渴望被他人看见的念头？

EN| The same way that a child would.
ZH| 就像小孩子那样。

EN| And what do we do when we want others to perceive us a certain way?
ZH| 那么，当我们渴望以某种姿态被他人看见时，我们会怎么做？

EN| We try to mold ourselves accordingly.
ZH| 我们会努力把自己塑造成那个样子。

EN| Ah!
ZH| 啊！

EN| I think I get it now!
ZH| 我想我懂了！

EN| The universe gradually felt the desire to be seen.
ZH| 宇宙渐渐萌生了渴望被看见的念头。

EN| It wanted to be admired, loved and observed, because there’s nothing more a world can do but to observe itself.
ZH| 它渴望被欣赏、被爱、被凝视——因为一个世界除了凝视自己，也再无事可做。

EN| It’s the best and only pastime there is, as far as the world is concerned.
ZH| 对世界而言，这是它最好、也是唯一的消遣。

EN| And if observation is good, more observation is even better!
ZH| 既然观察这么美好，那观察得越多自然越好！

EN| Of course, an endless universe can observe itself endlessly.
ZH| 当然，无穷的宇宙可以无穷地自我观察。

EN| You can say that its observation value equals infinity.
ZH| 可以说，它的“观察值”等于无穷大。

EN| That value may seem absolute, but it isn’t really, not in mathematics.
ZH| 这个值看似绝对，其实不然——在数学上并非如此。

EN| “Infinity plus one” exists.
ZH| “无穷大加一”是存在的。

EN| It’s a value that the universe could never reach as a singular, albeit infinite observer.
ZH| 凭它一个再无穷无尽的观察者，也永远够不到这个值。

EN| So it had to create new, finite observers to add to its infinity - it needed to create sentient life.
ZH| 于是它必须创造新的、有限的观察者，来为它的无穷添上一笔——它需要创造有意识的生命。

EN| Sentient life is the plus one!
ZH| 智慧生命，就是那个“加一”！

EN| When I put it like that, it sounds like all of us are extras at a party...
ZH| 这么一说，倒显得我们全是派对上的群演……

EN| Now isn't that fun!
ZH| 多有趣呀，不是吗！

EN| This vast mighty universe changed itself so meticulously to make new observers possible, it fine-tuned all of its systems so precisely...
ZH| 这浩瀚威严的宇宙，为了孕育新的观察者，如此细致地改变着自己，把它的一切系统都调校得如此精准……

EN| just to have us here.
ZH| 就为了让我们存在于世。

EN| It worked so hard just to share its one joy with us - us, worthless insects...!
ZH| 它如此辛苦，只为与我们分享它唯一的喜悦——与我们这些一文不值的虫豸……！

EN| Oh no...
ZH| 不好……

EN| Oh no, this can’t be...!
ZH| 不好、不会吧……！

EN| That’s cute!
ZH| 这也太可爱了！

EN| That’s really cute!
ZH| 真的太可爱了！

EN| Ah, I should’ve expected this.
ZH| 唉，我早该料到的。

EN| I should’ve known the infinite universe would be infinitely cute.
ZH| 无穷的宇宙自然是无限可爱，这还用说嘛。

EN| This world truly is wonderful, wonderful, wonderful!
ZH| 这个世界真是太美好了，美好、太美好了！

EN| Ira Grachevskaya is missing.
ZH| 伊拉・格拉乔夫斯卡娅失踪了。

EN| Huh?
ZH| 诶？

EN| I look up from my desk.
ZH| 我从课桌上抬起头。

EN| A low hum runs through the class.
ZH| 教室里响起一阵低低的骚动。

EN| Grachevskaya?
ZH| 格拉乔夫斯卡娅？

EN| What?
ZH| 什么？

EN| Seriously?
ZH| 真的假的？

EN| She’s just skipping...
ZH| 她不过是逃课了吧……

EN| Nah, she ran off with that one guy.
ZH| 不是吧，她是跟那个男人跑了。

EN| You saw?
ZH| 你看见了？

EN| No, but come on! You know her, she's totally-
ZH| 那倒没有，可你也懂她的嘛，她绝对就是——

EN| {cps=0}{size=+5}Silence!
ZH| {cps=0}{size=+5}安静！

EN| The teacher taps her journal on the desk.
ZH| 老师把点名册往桌上一敲。

EN| Ira’s parents filed the report yesterday night.
ZH| 伊拉的父母昨晚报了案。

EN| They haven’t seen her since she left for school.
ZH| 她早上出门上学之后，就再没人见过她。

EN| Another round of whispers.
ZH| 教室里又起一阵窃窃私语。

EN| The teacher raises her voice.
ZH| 老师提高了嗓门。

EN| So if any of you have any information that might help in finding Ira, please report it to the teacher’s office or the police after class.
ZH| 所以，如果你们中有谁知道任何能帮忙找到伊拉的线索，下课后请到教师办公室报告，或者告诉警察。

EN| That is all.
ZH| 就说这些。

EN| Onto the lesson.
ZH| 开始上课。

EN| A chair creaks in the back.
ZH| 后排传来椅子吱呀一声。

EN| Wait!
ZH| 等一下！

EN| You really expect us to just study like normal after hearing that?
ZH| 听完这种事，你还真指望我们跟没事人一样上课？

EN| Yeah!
ZH| 就是！

EN| Yeah!! We’re shocked, you know.
ZH| 就是！！我们可受惊了，你知道吧。

EN| We’re worried sick!
ZH| 我们担心死了！

EN| Seriously, our classmate’s missing.
ZH| 讲真，我们同学失踪了诶。

EN| Our friend, our Irochka!
ZH| 我们的朋友，我们的伊罗奇卡！

EN| The room fills with repulsive giggles.
ZH| 教室里充满令人作呕的窃笑。

EN| Cut it out.
ZH| 住口。

EN| Garin, sit back down.
ZH| 加林，坐回去。

EN| The teacher’s voice sounds just as dry as it did when she broke the news.
ZH| 老师的声音听起来和她宣布消息时一样干巴巴的。

EN| She turns towards the blackboard to write down the date.
ZH| 她转身面向黑板，写下日期。

EN| We all know the situation will probably...
ZH| 我们都知道，情况多半会……

EN| resolve itself just fine.
ZH| 自己就圆满解决了。

EN| So let’s not act dramatic.
ZH| 所以别在这里演戏。

EN| If you have anything valuable to say, I’ll be happy to hear it in the office.
ZH| 你们要是真有什么有价值的线索，我很乐意在办公室里听。

EN| She puts the chalk down and begins the lesson.
ZH| 她放下粉笔，开始讲课。

EN| \nIt’s December 19th.
ZH| \n这一天是12月19日。

EN| A bird flies past the window.
ZH| 一只鸟掠过窗外。

EN| Disappearance, gimme a break!
ZH| 失踪？饶了我吧！

EN| Aren't you supposed to wait three days or something?
ZH| 不是说要先等三天什么的吗？

EN| That’s a myth.
ZH| 那是讹传。

EN| You can file a missing report whenever, 'specially if it’s a kid.
ZH| 什么时候报案都行，尤其是小孩。

EN| Oh yeah?
ZH| 哦，是吗？

EN| Hey, let’s report each other as missing and see who they find first.
ZH| 嘿，要不咱们互相报失踪，看警察先找到谁。

EN| Ha, you're on!
ZH| 哈，一言为定！

EN| No, but seriously... what's with the sudden hubbub?
ZH| 不是，说真的……突然这么大惊小怪干嘛？

EN| So she didn't come home - big deal!
ZH| 不就是没回家嘛——有什么大不了的！

EN| I thought wandering off was her whole thing.
ZH| 到处乱晃不就是她的老本行吗。

ZH| 我会告诉所有人，我来自沃尔库塔，而不是沃尔库塔-5。

ZH| 而且那个身形也不像男人。

ZH| 毡靴陷进雪里，几乎没有阻力，几乎没有声音。

ZH| 我小小地向前迈了一步。

ZH| 我想说点什么，说什么都好——可大脑一片空白。

ZH| 还没等我挤出半个字，伊拉转过身，跌跌撞撞地踏着雪离开了游乐场。

ZH| 我连喊住她的勇气都挤不出来，更别说追上去。

EN| I simply watch as Ira vanishes into the alley.
ZH| 我只是眼睁睁看着伊拉的身影消失在小巷里。

EN| If not for her footprints, I’d believe she was never even there.
ZH| 要不是雪地上留着她的脚印，我简直会相信她从未来过。

EN| The snow keeps falling.
ZH| 雪还在下。

EN| A feeling I’ve long forgotten swells inside me.
ZH| 一种早已被遗忘的情感在我心中翻涌。

EN| I’ve learned to not acknowledge it, so it got buried many layers deep.
ZH| 我学会了不去承认它，所以它被深埋在层层叠叠之下。

EN| The hollow space inside my chest grows darker.
ZH| 我胸口里那片空洞，变得更暗了。

EN| The sky is full of pinpricks.
ZH| 天空缀满细小的星点。

EN| The world is looking down on me, but not in the way other people do.
ZH| 这个世界正俯视着我，但不是以其他人的那种方式。

EN| It’s kind and gentle, and I don’t feel small under its gaze.
ZH| 它善良而温柔，在它的注视下，我不觉得自己渺小。

EN| Its eyes are pure and tender.
ZH| 它的眼睛纯净而温暖。

第二章·第三天 (ep2d3.rpy)

EN| The world is as bright as a sunless day allows.
ZH| 在无太阳的白昼所允许的范围内，世界亮堂堂的。

EN| The air is clear and soft despite the cold.
ZH| 尽管寒冷，空气却清澈而柔和。

EN| The statue on the main square shines in silver.
ZH| 主广场上的雕像闪着银光。

EN| I have already made my rounds around the city.
ZH| 我已经把城里逛了一遍。

EN| It's been a wonderful time, as usual.
ZH| 和往常一样，度过了一段美好的时光。

EN| I've seen all that there is to see, I've admired all there is to admire...
ZH| 该看的都看了，该欣赏的都欣赏了……

EN| is what I would say, if I were \na liar!
ZH| 这话要是\n出自骗子之口就好了！

EN| I travel south, towards the single place I've been neglecting.
ZH| 我向南走去，前往我一直回避的唯一一处。

EN| It's easy to avoid, since it's right on the edge of the long residential street.
ZH| 它很容易避开，因为它就在那条长长的住宅街的尽头。

EN| The car repair shop.
ZH| 汽车修理厂。

EN| There's nothing special about it at all, aside from the fact that the owner's son is a classmate of mine.
ZH| 它毫无特别之处，除了厂主的儿子是我同学这一点。

EN| So what's my problem with it?
ZH| 那我到底在别扭什么？

EN| I suppose you can call it pettiness.
ZH| 大概可以称之为小气吧。

EN| Ever since that one incident, I've been avoiding the spot like the plague.
ZH| 自从那次事件之后，我一直像躲瘟疫一样躲着那个地方。

EN| But it was never the shop's fault.
ZH| 可那从来都不是修理厂的错。

EN| It was poor luck - that, and my own overreaction.
ZH| 只是运气不好——再加上我自己反应过度。

EN| If I want to achieve my goal, I need to get over myself and face it.
ZH| 如果想达成目标，我就得战胜自己，直面它。

EN| That's right, I'm sticking to it!
ZH| 没错，我的决心已定！

EN| I'm sending my message to the universe!
ZH| 我要向宇宙发送我的讯号！

EN| Of course, there are requirements I have to meet.
ZH| 当然，有几项要求必须达成。

EN| I need to take in and process the beauty of the whole entire world to achieve the right signal strength.
ZH| 我需要领略并消化整个世界的全部美好，才能达到应有的信号强度。

EN| If I can’t find the beauty in everything this town has to offer, how can I ever grasp the grandeur of the planet, let alone the universe?
ZH| 如果连这座小镇的美好都无法尽数发现，我又如何领略这颗星球的壮丽，更遑论宇宙？

EN| I’m well familiar with every other part of Vorkuta-5.
ZH| 沃尔库塔-5的其他地方我都熟得很。

EN| So excluding one area because of an old joke gone wrong is just unfair.
ZH| 所以仅仅因为一个开砸了的旧玩笑就排除一处，实在不公平。

EN| I’m sorry, car repair shop...
ZH| 对不起，汽车修理厂……

EN| I’ll fix my mistake.
ZH| 我会弥补我的过错。

EN| Ah-
ZH| 啊——

EN| I momentarily freeze up, then scoot behind a corner.
ZH| 我一瞬间僵住，然后闪身躲到墙角后。

EN| Just now...
ZH| 就在刚才……

EN| Was it Garin walking down that side of the road?
ZH| 从路那边走过来的是加林？

EN| I peek out just to check.
ZH| 我探头确认了一下。

EN| That’s right.
ZH| 没错。

EN| He’s marching quickly, hands in pockets.
ZH| 他正快步走着，双手插兜。

EN| A couple times he turns around as if to make sure he’s not being followed.
ZH| 有几次他回过头，像是在确认没人跟踪。

EN| Huh.
ZH| 咦。

EN| I wait until he is a good distance away before I come out of my hiding.
ZH| 我等到他走远才从藏身处出来。

EN| I’d rather not encounter him after what happened yesterday...
ZH| 经历了昨天的事，我可不想撞见他……

EN| Yeesh.
ZH| 啧。

EN| He’s never been this crude with me before.
ZH| 他从没对我这么粗鲁过。

EN| I mean, there are plenty of people on the street, so maybe I’m being overly cautious.
ZH| 再说街上有不少人，也许我是过度谨慎了。

EN| But better safe than sorry, right?
ZH| 但小心驶得万年船，对吧？

第二章·第六天 (ep2d6.rpy)

EN| Imagine drawing a line.
ZH| 想象你画下一条线。

EN| \n\nIt doesn’t have to be perfectly straight – in all likelihood, it won’t be.
ZH| \n\n它不必笔直——多半也不会笔直。

EN| If you go on for long enough, it will get wavy, unstable or jagged.
ZH| 画得够久，它就会起伏、摇摆或毛糙。

EN| After a certain point, even an artist’s steady hand can’t stay completely still.
ZH| 过了某个点，就算是画家的稳手也无法保持完全静止。

EN| \n\nBut let’s say you keep going on and on in one direction.
ZH| \n\n但假设你朝一个方向一直画下去。

EN| \n\nYou stare at the line you’ve drawn from up close and it looks like a mess.
ZH| \n\n凑近看你画出的线，它像一团乱麻。

EN| Wow!
ZH| 哇！

EN| So much variety, the rises, the falls, the soft waves, the sharp edges...
ZH| 起伏、坠落、柔波、锐角……变化多丰富啊。

EN| \n\n\n\n\nAnd then you zoom out.
ZH| \n\n\n\n\n然后你把镜头拉远。

EN| The further you go, the more insignificant those little fluctuations become.
ZH| 拉得越远，那些微小的波动就越微不足道。

EN| Once you rise way up high, your eyesight won’t distinguish them at all.
ZH| 升到足够高处，你的肉眼就再也分辨不出它们。

EN| \n\nAll you will see is a straight line.
ZH| \n\n你看到的，只剩一条直线。

EN| \n\n\n\nA single, perfect stroke.
ZH| \n\n\n\n一道完美的、笔直的笔画。

ZH| 你也许会说：万一波动巨大到占据了这条线的大半呢？

EN| What if the shift is so monumental, it can’t be ignored even when viewed from outer space?
ZH| 假如偏移如此剧烈，从外太空都清晰可见呢？

EN| \n\nEasy.
ZH| \n\n简单。

EN| The line needs to be longer.
ZH| 把线画得更长就行了。

EN| \n\nAnd past a certain point, all deviations – even ones spanning thousands of light years – will become negligible.
ZH| \n\n而过了某个临界点，一切偏差——哪怕绵延数千光年——都会变得微不足道。

EN| \n\n\n\nDo you see what I mean?
ZH| \n\n\n\n你明白我的意思了吗？

EN| Now picture a line drawn across the whole entire universe.
ZH| 现在，想象一条贯穿整个宇宙的线。

EN| A truly endless line.
ZH| 一条真正无始无终的线。

EN| \n\nWhat kind of curve could possibly put a dent in it?
ZH| \n\n什么样的曲线能令它凹陷？

EN| \n\nAn infinite line could only be offset by an infinite curve.
ZH| \n\n无限长的线，只能被无限的曲线偏移。

EN| \n\n\n\nAn infinite curve...
ZH| \n\n\n\n无限的曲线……

EN| \n\nA circle.
ZH| \n\n一个圆。

EN| An endless wheel.
ZH| 一只无尽的轮。

EN| A cassette reel that plays everything that has and will ever happen.
ZH| 一盘磁带卷轴，播放着曾经发生与将要发生的一切。

EN| Is the song endless?
ZH| 这首歌是无尽的吗？

EN| Or is it stuck on loop?
ZH| 还是卡在循环里？

EN| \n\nThe reel is endless, hence there’s no need to rewind the tape.
ZH| \n\n卷轴无尽，所以无需倒带。

EN| It goes on seamlessly, forever.
ZH| 它天衣无缝地播放，直到永远。

EN| And it’s a beautiful song.
ZH| 而这是一首美丽的歌。

EN| It doesn’t need changing – not that it’s possible to change.
ZH| 它不需要改变——何况也不可能改变。

EN| \n\nIsn’t it silly to still yearn for it?
ZH| \n\n还痴心妄想，不是很可笑吗？

EN| To dream of altering something that’s already perfect?
ZH| 妄图修改已然完美之物？

EN| \n\n\n\nNo, silly isn’t the right word.
ZH| \n\n\n\n不，“可笑”并不准确。

EN| It’s blasphemous.
ZH| 那是亵渎。

EN| \n\nWho are you to think you could improve a masterpiece?
ZH| \n\n你算什么东西，竟以为能改进杰作？

EN| \nYou, a human!
ZH| \n还是说，你寻求的是相反的东西？

EN| Or do you seek the opposite?
ZH| 你想玷污它？

EN| Do you wish to deface it?
ZH| 想像对一具躯体施加伤口那样，对它施加改变？

EN| To inflict change like you’d inflict a wound upon a body?
ZH| \n\n\n\n多么卑劣。

EN| \n\n\n\nHow low.
ZH| 幸好，你企图摧毁的这具躯体，愈合得远比你想象的更快更好。

EN| Thankfully, the body that you’re looking to destroy heals much better and faster than you could imagine.
ZH| 幸好，你企图摧毁的这具躯体，愈合得远比你想象的更快更好。

EN| \n\nYou push the knife in – and you feel it all.
ZH| \n\n你把刀捅进去——而你感受着一切。

ZH| 你感受到刀锋撞上骨头。

EN| You see how the blood gushes out and drips down to the hilt.
ZH| 你看到鲜血涌出，顺着刀柄滴落。

ZH| 因为你一直都心知肚明。

ZH| 暮色用呆滞的神情俯视着我。

ZH| 但有个问题。

ZH| 也许，可以借由窗户取得联系。

ZH| 嗯。

ZH| 不，好像不行。

ZH| 我说了——喂！

ZH| 别那样戳我……

终章 (ep3.rpy)
============================================================
EN| “Today is a good day.”
ZH| “今天是美好的一天。”

EN| \n\nIsn’t that a silly sentence?
ZH| \n\n这句话是不是很傻？

EN| You hear it all the time, but no one seems to realise how dumb it sounds.
ZH| 这话大家总在说，却好像没人意识到它听上去有多蠢。

EN| \n\nI guess it’s one of those common tautologies, like “close proximity” or “end result”.
ZH| \n\n大概它属于那种常见的同义反复，像“近距离接近”或“最终结果”一样。

EN| It’s not that I’m irritated by it...
ZH| 倒也不是说这让我恼火……

EN| I actually find it rather sweet.
ZH| 其实我觉得它相当可爱。

EN| \n\nBut even so, you should remember that each day is inherently good.
ZH| \n\n但即便如此，你也该记住：每一天本身就都是好的。

EN| Even if you feel a bit under the weather, it’s a good day.
ZH| 哪怕你有点不舒服，今天也是好日子。
